# DLMF Chapter 4 acquisition: Elementary Functions

This acquisition covers mathematical definitions, numbered formulas, exact tables and
analytical assertions in prose from [DLMF Chapter 4](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4),
version 1.2.8, checked on 2026-10-24. Graphics, application notes, software listings
and purely numerical values are excluded. Of 531 numbered equation slots, 510 are
active and in scope; seven exact tables are also retained. Six numerical equations,
14 application equations and the retired 4.13.E3 slot are explicitly identified below.
The algebraic 4.15.E1–E3 remain in scope despite their placement in the graphics section.

The entry point is [DLMF/Elementary.lean](DLMF/Elementary.lean).
[MathlibCoverage.lean](DLMF/Elementary/MathlibCoverage.lean) records 208 checked
Mathlib/TauCeti pointers. Existing local results are reused at their original declarations.
New statements were acquired with 447 direct admissions; the first parallel proof round removes 102,
leaving 345. Computational reduction identities and the exact Bessel-root correspondence
were proved during acquisition. Links and proof statuses below come from the compiled
DLMF tag dump; a definition or a proof depending on an admitted construction is not
counted as an admission-independent theorem.

The second parallel proof round proved 92 more statements, leaving 253. The
third round removes **28**, leaving **225 direct admissions**. Its proofs include
all six boundary logarithm series, complex binomial/derivative/limit adapters,
complete inverse-function families, and exact exponential integrals. Four false
inverse-hyperbolic full-set acquisitions (4.38.E15/E18) are corrected and proved
using shared roots and paired sinh/cosh equations; explicit Lean counterexamples
record the former errors. The two arctanh boundary-series acquisitions (4.38.E5/E6)
were corrected on 2026-10-25 (unordered `HasSum` strictly inside/outside the unit circle,
natural-order partial sums on DLMF's full closed domains) and proved, removing two admissions.
Lambert W retains 43 admissions and generalized exponentials/logarithms nine.

## Domains, conventions and source corrections

- General logarithms and powers are sets of values. Principal logarithms use Mathlib's
  upper half-open argument convention; the closed value sets include both cut sides.
  Path statements include attainment and all absolutely continuous paths avoiding zero.
- Boundary logarithm series use convergence of natural partial sums. Conditional
  convergence is not represented by the stronger unordered `HasSum` predicate.
- The logarithmic ODE 4.7.E13 needs an arbitrary multiplicative integration constant.
  The power-value identity 4.8.E5 correlates logarithm sheets rather than freely
  multiplying independent principal branches.
- The bounds 4.5.E3 and 4.5.E14 use strict interiors, with their cutoff endpoints
  stated separately. Integer trig orthogonality excludes both matching and opposite
  frequencies. Squared and Riccati general-solution formulas retain constant equilibria.
- Gudermannian arccos and inverse-Gudermannian arcosh formulas keep their sign domains.
  Hyperbolic half-angle square roots use continuation with a correlated sign.
  The printed sign in 4.37.E11/E14 is corrected in the acquired acosh formulas;
  the source qualifications appear at those declarations.
- Removable values at zero are explicit for sinhc and the primitive 4.40.E9.
  Lambert branches are separate from Mathlib's real inverse and use an admitted
  branch-family existence construction; their derived definitions inherit that admission.
- The earlier Bernoulli/partial-fraction tag mix-ups are corrected: tan 4.19.E3,
  csc 4.19.E4 and csc partial fractions 4.22.E5. Existing upstream coverage moved
  from `Core/UpstreamElementary` to the new checked catalog, with a compatible import.

## [§4.6 Power Series](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.6)

## [§4.8 Identities](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.8)

- [4.8.E1](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.8.E1): [`DLMF.Elementary.generalLogValues_mul`](DLMF/Elementary/LogExpAnalysis.lean#L970) (proved). New local statement or definition; acquisitions with admissions remain explicitly marked in source.
- [4.8.E2](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.8.E2): [`DLMF.Elementary.closedLog_mul`](DLMF/Elementary/LogExpAnalysis.lean#L1061) (proved); Mathlib: [`Complex.log_mul`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Complex/Log.html#Complex.log_mul). DLMF closed cut, not Mathlib upper-side-only log at the lower strip edge.
- [4.8.E3](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.8.E3): [`DLMF.Elementary.generalLogValues_div`](DLMF/Elementary/LogExpAnalysis.lean#L985) (proved). New local statement or definition; acquisitions with admissions remain explicitly marked in source.
- [4.8.E4](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.8.E4): [`DLMF.Elementary.closedLog_div`](DLMF/Elementary/LogExpAnalysis.lean#L1069) (proved). Closed branch-compatible quotient retains both strip edges.
- [4.8.E5](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.8.E5): [`DLMF.Elementary.generalLogValues_zpow`](DLMF/Elementary/LogExpAnalysis.lean#L1000) (proved). Corrected independent-set interpretation: all integer shifts of n*log z, even n=0; n*Ln z alone misses branches.
- [4.8.E6](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.8.E6): [`DLMF.Elementary.closedLog_zpow`](DLMF/Elementary/LogExpAnalysis.lean#L1077) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.log_zpow_strip`](DLMF/Elementary/LogExpAnalysis.lean#L1011) (proved). Both closed cut form and ordinary half-open principal log form included.
- [4.8.E7](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.8.E7): [`DLMF.Elementary.closedLog_inv`](DLMF/Elementary/LogExpAnalysis.lean#L1086) (proved); Mathlib: [`Complex.log_inv`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Complex/Log.html#Complex.log_inv). Closed inversion exchanges the upper and lower cut values.
- [4.8.E8](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.8.E8): [`DLMF.Elementary.generalLogValues_exp`](DLMF/Elementary/LogExpAnalysis.lean#L1021) (proved). New local statement or definition; acquisitions with admissions remain explicitly marked in source.
- [4.8.E9](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.8.E9): [`DLMF.Elementary.closedLog_exp`](DLMF/Elementary/LogExpAnalysis.lean#L1093) (proved); Mathlib: [`Complex.log_exp`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Complex/Log.html#Complex.log_exp). DLMF lower strip edge uses closed logarithm values.
- [4.8.E10](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.8.E10): [`DLMF.Elementary.exp_generalLog`](DLMF/Elementary/LogExpBasic.lean#L554) (proved); Mathlib: [`Complex.exp_log`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Complex/Log.html#Complex.exp_log). New local statement or definition; acquisitions with admissions remain explicitly marked in source.
- [4.8.E11](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.8.E11): [`DLMF.Elementary.generalLogValues_power`](DLMF/Elementary/LogExpAnalysis.lean#L1028) (proved). New local statement or definition; acquisitions with admissions remain explicitly marked in source.
- [4.8.E12](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.8.E12): [`DLMF.Elementary.log_cpow_sheet`](DLMF/Elementary/LogExpAnalysis.lean#L1035) (proved). Ordinary principal log uses the unique half-open strip correction; equivalent closed values represented by closedLogValues.
- [4.8.E13](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.8.E13): Mathlib: [`Real.log_rpow`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Pow/Real.html#Real.log_rpow). exact for positive real base and real exponent.
- [4.8.E14](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.8.E14): Mathlib: [`Complex.cpow_add`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Pow/Complex.html#Complex.cpow_add). exact for nonzero base and principal powers.
- [4.8.E15](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.8.E15): [`DLMF.Elementary.closedPower_mul`](DLMF/Elementary/LogExpAnalysis.lean#L1098) (proved). Compatible closed principal values use the same exponent and strip-compatible base logarithms.
- [4.8.E16](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.8.E16): Mathlib: [`Complex.exp_add`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/Complex/Exponential.html#Complex.exp_add). exact.
- [4.8.E17](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.8.E17): [`DLMF.Elementary.exp_cpow_int`](DLMF/Elementary/LogExpAnalysis.lean#L1056) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.exp_cpow_strip`](DLMF/Elementary/LogExpAnalysis.lean#L1050) (proved). Full ordinary principal strip plus unrestricted integer outer exponent; closed edges recorded separately by closedLog_exp.

## [§4.16 Elementary Properties](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.16)

- [4.16.T1](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.16.T1): [`DLMF.Elementary.trig_quadrant_signs`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2955) (proved); Mathlib: [`Real.cos_pos_of_mem_Ioo`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/Basic.html#Real.cos_pos_of_mem_Ioo); Mathlib: [`Real.sin_pos_of_pos_of_lt_pi`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/Basic.html#Real.sin_pos_of_pos_of_lt_pi). All quadrants and six functions captured in one finite-index sign table; axes excluded.
- [4.16.T2](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.16.T2): [`DLMF.Elementary.trig_quarter_period_table`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2996) (proved); Mathlib: [`Complex.cos_add_pi`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/Basic.html#Complex.cos_add_pi); Mathlib: [`Complex.cos_add_pi_div_two`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/Basic.html#Complex.cos_add_pi_div_two); Mathlib: [`Complex.sin_add_pi`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/Basic.html#Complex.sin_add_pi); Mathlib: [`Complex.sin_add_pi_div_two`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/Basic.html#Complex.sin_add_pi_div_two). Quarter-period table plus existing reflection/full-period pointers covers every plus/minus entry; totalized reciprocal identities retain poles separately.
- [4.16.T3](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.16.T3): [`DLMF.Elementary.trig_interrelation_table`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L3023) (proved). All 36 algebraic entries captured under the table convention that all functions are nonnegative, finite and real. Positive sine/cosine enforces this convention.

- [4.19.E8](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.19.E8): [`DLMF.Elementary.hasSum_log_cos_bernoulli`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L413) (admitted). Full formula represented by the listed native/pointer/local declarations; partial pointers retain their explicit comments.

## [§4.23 Inverse Trigonometric Functions](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.23)

- [4.23.E1](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.23.E1): [`DLMF.Elementary.arcsin_general_integral`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L1194) (admitted). General values are sets of inverse images, with an explicit path and continuous square-root lift integral characterization away from endpoint branch points. Endpoints retained in principal values and exact table.
- [4.23.E2](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.23.E2): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccos_general_integral`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L1201) (admitted). Path characterization uses pi/2 minus the lifted zero-to-z integral, equivalent to the source z-to-1 normalization.
- [4.23.E3](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.23.E3): [`DLMF.Elementary.arctan_general_integral`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L1208) (admitted). Full formula represented by the listed native/pointer/local declarations; partial pointers retain their explicit comments.
- [4.23.E4](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.23.E4): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccsc_general_reciprocal`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L1291) (proved). Full formula represented by the listed native/pointer/local declarations; partial pointers retain their explicit comments.
- [4.23.E5](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.23.E5): [`DLMF.Elementary.arcsec_general_reciprocal`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L1304) (proved). Full formula represented by the listed native/pointer/local declarations; partial pointers retain their explicit comments.
- [4.23.E6](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.23.E6): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccot_general_reciprocal`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L1317) (proved). Full formula represented by the listed native/pointer/local declarations; partial pointers retain their explicit comments.
- [4.23.E7](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.23.E7): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccscPrincipal`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L1144) (definition). Principal complex arcsine definition off cuts; reciprocal zero is excluded from the cut domain, and bank values are separate.
- [4.23.E8](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.23.E8): [`DLMF.Elementary.arcsecPrincipal`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L1148) (definition). Principal complex arccosine definition off cuts; reciprocal zero is excluded.
- [4.23.E9](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.23.E9): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccotPrincipal`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L1152) (definition). Reuses Mathlib Complex.arctan at 1/z; the assigned totalized value at zero is not a DLMF bank value.
- [4.23.E10](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.23.E10): [`DLMF.Elementary.arcsin_principal_neg`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L1487) (proved); Mathlib: [`Real.arcsin_neg`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/Inverse.html#Real.arcsin_neg). Off-cut oddness plus separate two-bank limits; a principal branch is not treated as single-valued on both banks.
- [4.23.E11](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.23.E11): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccos_principal_neg`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L1499) (proved); Mathlib: [`Real.arccos_neg`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/Inverse.html#Real.arccos_neg). Off-cut reflection plus separate two-bank limits.
- [4.23.E12](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.23.E12): [`DLMF.Elementary.arctan_principal_neg`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L1507) (proved); Mathlib: [`Real.arctan_neg`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/Arctan.html#Real.arctan_neg). Full formula represented by the listed native/pointer/local declarations; partial pointers retain their explicit comments.
- [4.23.E13](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.23.E13): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccsc_principal_neg`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L1517) (proved). Full formula represented by the listed native/pointer/local declarations; partial pointers retain their explicit comments.
- [4.23.E14](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.23.E14): [`DLMF.Elementary.arcsec_principal_neg`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L1525) (proved). Full formula represented by the listed native/pointer/local declarations; partial pointers retain their explicit comments.
- [4.23.E15](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.23.E15): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccot_principal_neg`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L1533) (proved). Full formula represented by the listed native/pointer/local declarations; partial pointers retain their explicit comments.
- [4.23.E16](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.23.E16): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccosPrincipal`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L1140) (definition); Mathlib: [`Real.arccos`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/Inverse.html#Real.arccos). Full-line odd identities retained. Principal arccos(sech x)/arcsec(cosh x) forms require x>=0; reciprocal coth/csch forms exclude their poles.

Principal arcosh(sec x)/arcsech(cos x) forms require x>=0; signed arsinh/artanh forms retain the full open interval.

[`DLMF.Elementary.inverse_trig_exact_table`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L3206) (proved); Mathlib: [`Real.arccos_neg_one`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/Inverse.html#Real.arccos_neg_one); Mathlib: [`Real.arccos_one`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/Inverse.html#Real.arccos_one); Mathlib: [`Real.arccos_zero`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/Inverse.html#Real.arccos_zero); Mathlib: [`Real.arcsin_neg_one`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/Inverse.html#Real.arcsin_neg_one); Mathlib: [`Real.arcsin_one`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/Inverse.html#Real.arcsin_one); Mathlib: [`Real.arcsin_zero`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/Inverse.html#Real.arcsin_zero); Mathlib: [`Real.arctan_one`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/Arctan.html#Real.arctan_one); Mathlib: [`Real.arctan_zero`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/Arctan.html#Real.arctan_zero); Mathlib: [`Real.tendsto_arctan_atBot`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/Arctan.html#Real.tendsto_arctan_atBot); Mathlib: [`Real.tendsto_arctan_atTop`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/Arctan.html#Real.tendsto_arctan_atTop). All finite real entries, both arccot banks at zero, and reciprocal limits at ±infinity retained. Undefined real cells are not assigned principal real values.

## [§4.24 Inverse Trigonometric Functions: Further Properties](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.24)

- [4.24.E1](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.24.E1): [`DLMF.Elementary.hasSum_arcsin_principal`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2451) (admitted). Closed unit disc, including both endpoints, retained.
- [4.24.E2](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.24.E2): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccos_series_center`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2464) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.hasSum_arccos_principal`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2457) (admitted). Square-root prefactor is inside the HasSum so the removable center z=1 is meaningful; full closed radius two retained.
- [4.24.E3](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.24.E3): [`DLMF.Elementary.hasSum_arctan_closed_disc`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2473) (admitted); Mathlib: [`Complex.hasSum_arctan`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Complex/Arctan.html#Complex.hasSum_arctan). Uses ordered partial sums on the closed unit disc because convergence at generic boundary points is conditional; ±i excluded.
- [4.24.E4](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.24.E4): [`DLMF.Elementary.arctan_exterior_series`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2480) (admitted). Ordered exterior series, correct sign of the pi/2 term, nonzero real part.
- [4.24.E5](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.24.E5): [`DLMF.Elementary.hasSum_arctan_euler`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2487) (admitted). Euler series has the z/(1+z²) prefactor inside the sum, preserving z=0.
- [4.24.E6](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.24.E6): [`DLMF.Elementary.arctan_euler_boundary`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L3622) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.arctan_euler_domain`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2494) (proved). Full formula represented by the listed native/pointer/local declarations; partial pointers retain their explicit comments.
- [4.24.E7](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.24.E7): [`DLMF.Elementary.hasDerivAt_arcsin_principal`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2499) (proved); Mathlib: [`Real.hasDerivAt_arcsin`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/InverseDeriv.html#Real.hasDerivAt_arcsin). Full formula represented by the listed native/pointer/local declarations; partial pointers retain their explicit comments.
- [4.24.E8](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.24.E8): [`DLMF.Elementary.hasDerivAt_arccos_principal`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2505) (proved); Mathlib: [`Real.hasDerivAt_arccos`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/InverseDeriv.html#Real.hasDerivAt_arccos). Full formula represented by the listed native/pointer/local declarations; partial pointers retain their explicit comments.
- [4.24.E9](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.24.E9): [`DLMF.Elementary.hasDerivAt_arctan_principal`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2511) (proved); Mathlib: [`Real.hasDerivAt_arctan`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/ArctanDeriv.html#Real.hasDerivAt_arctan). Full formula represented by the listed native/pointer/local declarations; partial pointers retain their explicit comments.
- [4.24.E10](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.24.E10): [`DLMF.Elementary.hasDerivAt_arccsc_principal`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2642) (proved). Principal derivative sign is stated on each nonzero-real-part component, as in source Re z greater/less than zero.
- [4.24.E11](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.24.E11): [`DLMF.Elementary.hasDerivAt_arcsec_principal`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2660) (proved). Principal derivative sign is stated on each nonzero-real-part component.
- [4.24.E12](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.24.E12): [`DLMF.Elementary.hasDerivAt_arccot_principal`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2668) (proved). Full formula represented by the listed native/pointer/local declarations; partial pointers retain their explicit comments.
- [4.24.E13](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.24.E13): [`DLMF.Elementary.arcsin_general_add`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2682) (proved). Literal independent-root converse-set equality is false (u=v=1 gives lhs pi+2piZ, rhs Arcsin(0)=piZ). Correct branch-compatible membership identity is proved.
- [4.24.E14](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.24.E14): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccos_general_add`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2692) (proved). Full formula represented by the listed native/pointer/local declarations; partial pointers retain their explicit comments.
- [4.24.E15](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.24.E15): [`DLMF.Elementary.arctan_general_add`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2764) (proved). Finite quotient requires 1-sign*u*v nonzero; ±i inputs excluded.
- [4.24.E16](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.24.E16): [`DLMF.Elementary.arcsin_arccos_general_add`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2808) (proved). Square-root signs are correlated with the selected inverse values; independent-root inverse-image set converses can add branches. Correct compatible-branch identities are proved.
- [4.24.E17](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.24.E17): [`DLMF.Elementary.arctan_arccot_general_add`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2836) (proved). Both displayed finite quotients require nonzero denominators; ±i inputs excluded.

## [§4.25 Continued Fractions](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.25)

- [4.25.E1](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.25.E1): [`DLMF.Elementary.tan_continued_fraction`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2899) (admitted). Continued-fraction convergence uses continuants and excludes cosine poles.
- [4.25.E2](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.25.E2): [`DLMF.Elementary.tan_scaled_continued_fraction`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2906) (admitted). All complex a retained; exact open real strip and target tangent nonpole condition.
- [4.25.E3](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.25.E3): [`DLMF.Elementary.arcsin_continued_fraction`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2914) (admitted). Open principal arcsine cut plane; all specified numerator repetitions retained.
- [4.25.E4](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.25.E4): [`DLMF.Elementary.arctan_continued_fraction`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2922) (admitted). Open principal arctangent cut plane.
- [4.25.E5](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.25.E5): [`DLMF.Elementary.exp_arccot_continued_fraction`](DLMF/Elementary/Trigonometric.lean#L2929) (admitted). All complex a, open arccotangent cut plane, exp minus one is the continued-fraction tail.

Source integer-frequency claim with m!=n alone is false at m=-n. Correct acquisition also assumes m!=-n; TauCeti provides the natural-frequency case.

Full formula represented by the listed native/pointer/local declarations; partial pointers retain their explicit comments.

## [§4.28 Definitions and Periodicity](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.28)

- [4.28.E2](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.28.E2): Mathlib: [`Complex.cosh`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/Complex/Trigonometric.html#Complex.cosh). exact: complex exponential definition.
- [4.28.E3](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.28.E3): Mathlib: [`Complex.cosh_add_sinh`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/Complex/Trigonometric.html#Complex.cosh_add_sinh); Mathlib: [`Complex.cosh_sub_sinh`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/Complex/Trigonometric.html#Complex.cosh_sub_sinh). exact: upper sign; exact: lower sign.
- [4.28.E4](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.28.E4): Mathlib: [`Complex.tanh`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/Complex/Trigonometric.html#Complex.tanh); Mathlib: [`Complex.tanh_eq_sinh_div_cosh`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/Complex/Trigonometric.html#Complex.tanh_eq_sinh_div_cosh). exact.
- [4.28.E5](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.28.E5): [`DLMF.Elementary.csch`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L49) (definition). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.
- [4.28.E6](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.28.E6): [`DLMF.Elementary.sech`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L52) (definition). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.
- [4.28.E7](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.28.E7): [`DLMF.Elementary.coth`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L55) (definition); [`DLMF.Elementary.coth_eq_inv_tanh`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L112) (proved). Coth is cosh/sinh with Lean totalizations; equality to inverse tanh is proved.
- [4.28.E8](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.28.E8): Mathlib: [`Complex.sin_mul_I`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/Complex/Trigonometric.html#Complex.sin_mul_I). exact: commute i and z.
- [4.28.E9](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.28.E9): Mathlib: [`Complex.cos_mul_I`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/Complex/Trigonometric.html#Complex.cos_mul_I). exact: commute i and z.
- [4.28.E10](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.28.E10): Mathlib: [`Complex.tan_mul_I`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/Complex/Trigonometric.html#Complex.tan_mul_I). exact: commute i and z.
- [4.28.E11](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.28.E11): [`DLMF.Elementary.csc_mul_I`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L117) (proved). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.
- [4.28.E12](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.28.E12): [`DLMF.Elementary.sec_mul_I`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L122) (proved). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.
- [4.28.E13](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.28.E13): [`DLMF.Elementary.cot_mul_I`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L127) (proved). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.

## [§4.30 Elementary Properties](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.30)

- [4.30.T1](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.30.T1): [`DLMF.Elementary.hyperbolic_interrelations_0`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L192) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.hyperbolic_interrelations_1`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L216) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.hyperbolic_interrelations_2`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L240) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.hyperbolic_interrelations_3`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L264) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.hyperbolic_interrelations_4`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L288) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.hyperbolic_interrelations_5`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L312) (proved). All36 exact algebraic entries acquired as six column-vector identities, with all six functions real, nonnegative and finite as in the caption. The pole exclusions in HyperbolicPositive prevent treating infinity as Lean zero.

## [§4.31 Special Values and Limits](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.31)

- [4.31.E1](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.31.E1): [`DLMF.Elementary.sinh_div_limit`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L336) (proved); Mathlib: [`Complex.isEquivalent_sinh`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/DerivHyp.html#Complex.isEquivalent_sinh). equivalent: sinh z is asymptotic to z at zero.
- [4.31.E2](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.31.E2): [`DLMF.Elementary.tanh_div_limit`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L341) (proved). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.
- [4.31.E3](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.31.E3): [`DLMF.Elementary.cosh_sub_one_div_sq_limit`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L351) (proved). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.
- [4.31.T1](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.31.T1): [`DLMF.Elementary.hyperbolic_real_infinity`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L407) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.hyperbolic_special_values`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L371) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.hyperbolic_table_cosh_poles`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L456) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.hyperbolic_table_imaginary_pole_sides`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L4426) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.hyperbolic_table_sinh_poles`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L438) (proved). All finite exact entries, real-infinity limits, meromorphic norm-pole limits, and the indicated one-sided imaginary-pole signs acquired. Pole entries are not assigned infinite complex values; vector zeros at poles only record the independent Lean totalizations.

- [4.34.E9](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.34.E9): [`DLMF.Elementary.hyperbolic_squared_ode_minus`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L860) (admitted). Connected complex-domain general solution; constant equilibria f=±1/a are explicitly included alongside the stated nonconstant family.

- [4.34.E11](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.34.E11): [`DLMF.Elementary.hyperbolic_linear_ode`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L787) (proved). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.

- [4.34.E13](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.34.E13): [`DLMF.Elementary.hyperbolic_squared_ode_minus`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L860) (admitted). The cosh family plus its missing constant equilibria; source general-solution wording is corrected.
- [4.34.E14](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.34.E14): [`DLMF.Elementary.hyperbolic_riccati_ode`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L869) (admitted). The coth family plus its missing constant equilibria±1/a; source general-solution wording is corrected.

## [§4.37 Inverse Hyperbolic Functions](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37)

- [4.37.E1](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E1): [`DLMF.Elementary.arcsinh_path_values`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L1514) (admitted). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.
- [4.37.E2](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E2): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccosh_path_values`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L1519) (admitted). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.
- [4.37.E3](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E3): [`DLMF.Elementary.arctanh_path_values`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L1524) (admitted). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.
- [4.37.E4](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E4): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccsch_values_inv`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L1529) (proved). Reciprocal multivalued inverse at z≠0; zero is singular and the inverse equation excludes totalized poles.
- [4.37.E5](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E5): [`DLMF.Elementary.arcsech_values_inv`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L1541) (proved). Reciprocal multivalued inverse at z≠0; zero is singular and the inverse equation excludes totalized poles.
- [4.37.E6](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E6): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccoth_values_inv`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L1553) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.arccoth_zero_values`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L4619) (proved). Reciprocal multivalued inverse at z≠0. At z=0 a separate half-period family is stated, preserving the limit through reciprocal infinity.
- [4.37.E7](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E7): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccsch`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L78) (definition). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.
- [4.37.E8](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E8): [`DLMF.Elementary.arcsech`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L81) (definition). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.
- [4.37.E9](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E9): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccoth_eq_inv`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L1575) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.arccoth_zero_values`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L4619) (proved). Principal reciprocal formula at z≠0 and the separate half-period family at z=0; principal arccoth0 chooses one of its two cut values.
- [4.37.E10](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E10): [`DLMF.Elementary.arcsinh_neg`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L2101) (proved); Mathlib: [`Real.arsinh_neg`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Arsinh.html#Real.arsinh_neg). Principal arcsinh oddness acquired for every complex argument, with cut sides recorded separately.
- [4.37.E11](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E11): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccosh_neg`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L2200) (proved). Confirmed source sign mismatch, corrected locally: arccosh(−z)=arccosh(z)−sgn(Im z)πi. E20 yields arccosh(i)=log(1+sqrt2)+πi/2 and arccosh(−i)=log(1+sqrt2)−πi/2, contradicting the printed + sign for Im z>0.
- [4.37.E12](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E12): [`DLMF.Elementary.arctanh_neg`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L2112) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.arctanh_values_neg`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L4584) (proved); TauCeti: [`Real.artanh_neg_eq_neg_artanh`](https://github.com/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/blob/5cdecf40e99d5a5ad0833afff31f5b007d68a149/TauCeti/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Artanh.lean#L84). Principal oddness on the open cut plane, oddness of the full inverse-value sets, and both cut-side limits preserve the two-valued cut convention.
- [4.37.E13](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E13): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccsch_neg`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L2122) (proved). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.
- [4.37.E14](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E14): [`DLMF.Elementary.arcsech_neg`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L2243) (proved). Reciprocation of the corrected E11 sign: arcsech(−z)=arcsech(z)+sgn(Im z)πi. The printed pairing is reversed relative to E20.
- [4.37.E15](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E15): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccoth_neg`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L2129) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.arccoth_values_neg`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L4595) (proved). Principal oddness on the open cut plane and oddness of the full inverse-value sets, including the two values at zero.
- [4.37.E16](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E16): [`DLMF.Elementary.arcsinh`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L70) (definition); [`DLMF.Elementary.arcsinh_log`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L2258) (proved); Mathlib: [`Real.arsinh`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Arsinh.html#Real.arsinh). special case: real arguments; complex principal branches acquired locally.
- [4.37.E17](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E17): [`DLMF.Elementary.arcsinh_upper_cut`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L2319) (proved). Both right/left one-sided limits, including the branch endpoint y=1.
- [4.37.E18](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E18): [`DLMF.Elementary.arcsinh_lower_cut`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L2360) (proved). Both right/left one-sided limits, including the branch endpoint y=−1.
- [4.37.E19](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E19): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccosh_log`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L2483) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.arccosh_one`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L4614) (proved); Mathlib: [`Real.arcosh`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Arcosh.html#Real.arcosh). Positive/negative-real-part log form off the cut, imaginary-axis formula in E20, and the finite endpoint at1 acquired.
- [4.37.E20](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E20): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccosh_imaginary`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L2489) (proved). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.
- [4.37.E21](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E21): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccosh_log_half`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L2574) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.arccosh_one`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L4614) (proved). Equivalent two-square-root log formula off the cut plus the endpoint at1.
- [4.37.E22](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E22): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccosh_inner_cut`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L2603) (proved). Both upper/lower banks of the inner cut acquired as one-sided limits.
- [4.37.E23](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E23): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccosh_outer_cut`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L2696) (proved). Both upper/lower banks of the outer cut acquired as one-sided limits.
- [4.37.E24](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E24): [`DLMF.Elementary.arctanh`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L75) (definition); [`DLMF.Elementary.arctanh_log`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L2787) (proved); Mathlib: [`Real.artanh_eq_half_log`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Artanh.html#Real.artanh_eq_half_log). special case: real arguments in [-1,1], endpoint totalizations distinguished.
- [4.37.E25](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E25): [`DLMF.Elementary.arctanh_cut`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L2792) (proved). Both upper/lower banks acquired as one-sided limits; no arbitrary single cut value asserted equal to both.
- [4.37.E26](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E26): [`DLMF.Elementary.sinh_inverse_family`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L2854) (proved). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.
- [4.37.E27](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E27): [`DLMF.Elementary.cosh_inverse_family`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L2861) (proved). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.
- [4.37.E28](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E28): [`DLMF.Elementary.tanh_inverse_family`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L2898) (proved). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.
- [4.37.E29](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E29): [`DLMF.Elementary.sinh_inverse_family`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L2854) (proved). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.
- [4.37.E30](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E30): [`DLMF.Elementary.cosh_inverse_family`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L2861) (proved). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.
- [4.37.E31](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.37.E31): [`DLMF.Elementary.tanh_inverse_family`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L2898) (proved). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.

## [§4.38 Inverse Hyperbolic Functions: Further Properties](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.38)

- [4.38.E1](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.38.E1): [`DLMF.Elementary.hasSum_arcsinh`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3107) (admitted). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.
- [4.38.E2](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.38.E2): [`DLMF.Elementary.hasSum_arcsinh_large`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3112) (admitted). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.
- [4.38.E3](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.38.E3): [`DLMF.Elementary.hasSum_arccosh_large`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3117) (admitted). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.
- [4.38.E4](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.38.E4): [`DLMF.Elementary.hasSum_arccosh_near_one`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3122) (admitted). Boundary of the convergence disk retained, including the removable normalized value1 at z=1.
- [4.38.E5](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.38.E5): [`DLMF.Elementary.hasSum_arctanh`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3215) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.tendsto_arctanh_series`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3207) (proved). **Corrected** (project transcription defect, 2026-10-25): the former unordered HasSum on the closed disk was false on the unit circle (z=i gives nonsummable term norms 1/(2n+1)); now unordered HasSum for |z|<1 and natural-order partial sums on DLMF's full domain |z|≤1, z≠±1, via the boundary odd logarithm series.
- [4.38.E6](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.38.E6): [`DLMF.Elementary.hasSum_arctanh_large`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3249) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.tendsto_arctanh_large_series`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3228) (proved). **Corrected** (project transcription defect, 2026-10-25): the former unordered HasSum for |z|≥1 was false on the unit circle (z=i gives nonsummable term norms 1/(2n+1)); now unordered HasSum for |z|>1 and natural-order partial sums on DLMF's full domain |z|≥1, Im z≷0, with the ±iπ/2 sign of Im z.
- [4.38.E7](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.38.E7): [`DLMF.Elementary.hasSum_arctanh_transformed`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3266) (admitted). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.
- [4.38.E8](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.38.E8): [`DLMF.Elementary.arctanh_transformed_boundary`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L4608) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.arctanh_transformed_region`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3271) (proved). Both strict interior-region and equality boundary-hyperbola forms acquired.
- [4.38.E9](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.38.E9): [`DLMF.Elementary.hasDerivAt_arcsinh`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3277) (proved); Mathlib: [`Real.hasDerivAt_arsinh`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Arsinh.html#Real.hasDerivAt_arsinh). special case: real arguments.
- [4.38.E10](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.38.E10): [`DLMF.Elementary.hasDerivAt_arccosh`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3283) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.hasDerivAt_arccosh_signed`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3288) (proved); Mathlib: [`Real.hasDerivAt_arcosh`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Arcosh.html#Real.hasDerivAt_arcosh). Signed principal-square-root derivative plus the factored-root derivative covering the uncut imaginary axis.
- [4.38.E11](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.38.E11): [`DLMF.Elementary.hasDerivAt_arctanh`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3296) (proved); TauCeti: [`Real.hasDerivAt_artanh`](https://github.com/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/blob/5cdecf40e99d5a5ad0833afff31f5b007d68a149/TauCeti/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Artanh.lean#L180). special case: real arguments in (-1,1); pinned TauCeti.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.Artanh.
- [4.38.E12](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.38.E12): [`DLMF.Elementary.hasDerivAt_arccsch`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3302) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.hasDerivAt_arccsch_signed`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3368) (proved). Signed square-root derivative for nonzero real part plus the full reciprocal-chain derivative covering the remaining uncut axis.
- [4.38.E13](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.38.E13): [`DLMF.Elementary.hasDerivAt_arcsech`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3378) (proved). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.
- [4.38.E14](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.38.E14): [`DLMF.Elementary.hasDerivAt_arccoth`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3391) (proved). Complex-domain statement acquired with explicit branches, convergence and pole conditions.
- [4.38.E15](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.38.E15): [`DLMF.Elementary.arcsinh_values_add`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3425) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.arcsinh_values_sub`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3721) (proved). Exact multivalued sum/difference sets with shared square roots and both sinh/cosh equations, including critical parameters. **Acquisition corrected and proved:** shared roots and paired sinh/cosh equations retain branch compatibility; explicit Lean counterexamples cover the former independent-root formulation.
- [4.38.E16](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.38.E16): [`DLMF.Elementary.arccosh_values_add`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3455) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.arccosh_values_sub`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3750) (proved). Set equality for all multivalued inverse sums/differences; both square-root values are quantified.
- [4.38.E17](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.38.E17): [`DLMF.Elementary.arctanh_values_add`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3577) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.arctanh_values_sub`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3783) (proved); TauCeti: [`Real.artanh_add`](https://github.com/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/blob/5cdecf40e99d5a5ad0833afff31f5b007d68a149/TauCeti/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Artanh.lean#L132); TauCeti: [`Real.artanh_sub`](https://github.com/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/blob/5cdecf40e99d5a5ad0833afff31f5b007d68a149/TauCeti/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Artanh.lean#L154). Full multivalued sum/difference identities where the displayed rational argument is finite; singular denominator cases are not assigned totalized values.
- [4.38.E18](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.38.E18): [`DLMF.Elementary.arcsinh_arccosh_values_add`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3640) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.arcsinh_arccosh_values_sub`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3809) (proved). Both displayed multivalued inverse expressions, both signs and every square-root value are retained. **Acquisition corrected and proved:** both formulations use shared square roots and paired sinh/cosh equations; the former uncorrelated full sets fail at u=i,v=1.
- [4.38.E19](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.38.E19): [`DLMF.Elementary.arctanh_arccoth_values_add`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3665) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.arctanh_arccoth_values_sub`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3834) (proved). Both multivalued inverse expressions and both signs, with finite-argument exclusions for each rational expression.

## [§4.39 Continued Fractions](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.39)

- [4.39.E1](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.39.E1): [`DLMF.Elementary.tanh_continued_fraction`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3875) (admitted). Convergence of finite continuants, excluding cosh zeros, rather than equality to a totalized infinite fraction.
- [4.39.E2](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.39.E2): [`DLMF.Elementary.arcsinh_continued_fraction`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3881) (admitted). Convergence of finite continuants on the complete open arcsinh cut plane, repeated numerator pairs preserved.
- [4.39.E3](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.39.E3): [`DLMF.Elementary.arctanh_continued_fraction`](DLMF/Elementary/Hyperbolic.lean#L3889) (admitted). Convergence of finite continuants on the complete open arctanh cut plane.

## Analytical prose and exclusions

The paragraph links record mathematical assertions and conventions as well as explicit exclusions.
A bibliographic pointer with no stated formula is not converted into an opaque axiom.

Literal independent-root converse-set equality is false (u=v=1 gives lhs pi+2piZ, rhs Arcsin(0)=piZ). Correct branch-compatible membership identity is proved. Square-root signs are correlated with the selected inverse values; independent-root inverse-image set converses can add branches. Correct compatible-branch identities are proved.

- [4.45.Px1.p1](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.45#Px1.p1): [`DLMF.Elementary.logReductionArgument`](DLMF/Elementary/Computation.lean#L30) (definition); [`DLMF.Elementary.logReductionArgument_tendsto`](DLMF/Elementary/Computation.lean#L34) (proved). Repeated-root logarithm reduction and convergence.
- [4.45.Px1.p2](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.45#Px1.p2): Mathlib: [`Real.log_mul`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Log/Basic.html#Real.log_mul). Decimal logarithm scaling is recorded among the numbered formulas.
- [4.45.Px2.p1](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.45#Px2.p1): [`DLMF.Elementary.expReductionArgument_bound`](DLMF/Elementary/Computation.lean#L74) (proved). Exact exponential reduction bound; decimal approximation excluded: numerical.
- [4.45.Px3.p1](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.45#Px3.p1): [`DLMF.Elementary.trigReductionAngle_bound`](DLMF/Elementary/Computation.lean#L80) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.trigReductionAngle_eq`](DLMF/Elementary/Computation.lean#L86) (proved). Exact nearest-integer angle reduction, including the boundary bound; decimal approximation excluded: numerical.
- [4.45.Px3.p2](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.45#Px3.p2): Reciprocal definitions already covered by4.14.E4–E7.
- [4.45.Px4.p1](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.45#Px4.p1): [`DLMF.Elementary.arctanReductionSequence_identity`](DLMF/Elementary/Computation.lean#L169) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.arctanReductionSequence_le`](DLMF/Elementary/Computation.lean#L146) (proved); [`DLMF.Elementary.arctanReductionSequence_tendsto`](DLMF/Elementary/Computation.lean#L159) (proved). Exact iteration, geometric contraction and convergence to zero.
- [4.45.Px4.p2](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.45#Px4.p2): The convergent reciprocal arctangent series is covered by4.45.E11.
- [4.45.Px4.p3](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.45#Px4.p3): excluded: numerical.
- [4.45.Px4.p4](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.45#Px4.p4): excluded: numerical.
- [4.45.Px4.p5](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.45#Px4.p5): excluded: numerical.
- [4.45.Px4.p6](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.45#Px4.p6): Mathlib: [`Real.arcsin_eq_arctan`](https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Analysis/SpecialFunctions/Trigonometric/Arctan.html#Real.arcsin_eq_arctan). Inverse sine/arctangent conversion with its open real domain; reciprocal conversions are recorded in 4.16.T3.
- [4.45.Px5.p1](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.45#Px5.p1): Computational use of the acquired definitions and branch-qualified logarithmic forms; no additional formula.
- [4.45.Px6.p1](https://dlmf.nist.gov/4.45#Px6.p1): Bibliographic reference without an additional analytical assertion.

## Validation

The ordinary `lake build` and strict checks with `autoImplicit=false` and
`relaxedAutoImplicit=false` pass for all changed Lean files and both entry modules.
Compiled tag and theorem-axiom audits distinguish direct admissions from inherited
dependencies and report no axiom beyond `sorryAx` and the standard three.
The numbered source inventory has no unexplained in-scope gap.
